*Deaths/Tick derived: (initial + recruited − remaining) / ticks — assumes units only leave via combat death. Gate counters: occupancy-gate scans per tick in movement; gate-blocked = movers whose target was occupied; cooldown-skipped = blocked movers in their 3-tick backoff (W1-④). Supply-Blocked = units whose supply delivery was denied by the player's funds / manpower pool (FCFS gate; see city:unit ratio caveat).

Network Phase

Sub-phases of the per-tick network phase (splice encoder off in the benchmark). net = the whole tick_system!-wrapped phase (snapshot + delta + encode + baseline commit); the sub-rows are the measured parts, so their sum is ≤ net. send is always 0: the headless benchmark encodes in memory and models no outbox/WS bridge. ms/Tick = average over all 4500 measured ticks.

Sub-phasems/Tick (avg)
0.632 0.038 0.023 0.525 0.000

Idle / Movement × Grid-Clean / Grid-Dirty

Per-regime averages over measured ticks. Regime = movers == 0 (idle) vs movers > 0 (movement); grid-dirty = a vision/border grid diff was emitted that tick (influence recompute makes ~29% of ticks grid-dirty even at zero movers). Wire/allocs/bytes are per-tick averages within the regime.
